class BaseAgent(ABC)
  def __init__(
        self,
        agent_type: AgentType,
        name: Optional[str] = None,
        config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        logger: Optional[logging.Logger] = None
    )  # Initialize base agent
  def execute(self, query: AgentQuery) -> AgentResult  # Execute agent-specific logic (must be implemented by subclasses)
  def run(self, query: AgentQuery) -> AgentResult  # Main entry point for agent execution with error handling and timing
  def get_statistics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Get comprehensive agent performance statistics
  def get_performance_summary(self) -> str  # Get human-readable performance summary
  def reset_statistics(self) -> None  # Reset agent statistics including timing data
  def __repr__(self) -> str  # String representation of agent

        strategy_payload: Dict[str, Any],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Validate strategy comparison uses a sufficiently large, metadata-rich corpus.
  def _enforce_uncertainty_adjusted_utility_guard(
        self,
        ranking_comparison: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]],
    ) -> None  # Fail when concept-aware underperforms baseline on hard archetypes.
  def _validate_monotonic_safety_response(
        self,
        hard_archetype_profiles: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Validate step-radius contraction as regret/drift risk increases.
  def _validate_temporal_forgetting_safety(
        self,
        before: Dict[str, float],
        after: Dict[str, float],
        citation_validity_floor: float = 0.62,
        before_per_archetype: Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]] = None,
        after_per_archetype: Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Check forgetting schedule improves hard utility without citation harm.
  def _risk_score(
        utility: float,
        redundancy: float,
        drift_norm: float,
    ) -> float  # Compute normalized risk score from utility, redundancy and drift.

    ) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Learn step mapping from held-out hard-archetype trajectories.

    ) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Compute bootstrap delta intervals for hard-archetype utility uplift.

        target_grounding: List[float],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Detect drift indicating utility-weight recalibration is needed.

  def _bootstrap_confidence_interval(
        self,
        values: List[float],
        samples: Optional[int] = None,
        alpha: float = 0.05,
    ) -> Dict[str, float]  # Estimate confidence interval with bootstrap resampling.

        self,
        citation: Any,

        seen_concept_groups: set,
    ) -> Tuple[float, set]  # Compute per-citation utility from concept coverage, centrality, novelty.

  def _citation_metadata_completeness(citations: List[Any]) -> float  # Estimate completeness ratio for citation metadata fields.
  def _citation_metadata_quality(citations: List[Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Compute metadata quality, uniqueness, and source concentration diagnostics.
  def _benchmark_aggregation_strategies(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]  # Compare weighted/diversified/concept-aware across archetypes with macro metrics.

  def export_benchmark_results(
        self,

    )  # Export benchmark results to JSON file.

# src/eeg_rag/ingestion/pipeline.py [python]
imports: asyncio, dataclasses.dataclass, dataclasses.asdict, dataclasses.field, typing.Optional, typing.AsyncIterator, typing.Union, pathlib.Path, json, logging, datetime.datetime, hashlib, pubmed_client, scholar_client, arxiv_client, openalex_client, clinicaltrials_client, europepmc_client
class UnifiedDocument
  def __post_init__(self)
  def to_dict(self) -> dict  # Convert to dictionary for JSON serialization.
  def get_embedding_text(self) -> str  # Get text for embedding generation.
class IngestionPipeline
  def __init__(
        self,
        output_dir: str = "data/raw",
        pubmed_api_key: Optional[str] = None,
        semantic_scholar_api_key: Optional[str] = None,
        email: str = "your-email@example.com"
    )  # Initialize ingestion pipeline.
  def _normalize_title(self, title: str) -> str  # Normalize title for deduplication.
  def _generate_doc_id(self, doc: UnifiedDocument) -> str  # Generate unique document ID.
  def _is_duplicate(self, doc: UnifiedDocument) -> bool  # Check if document is a duplicate.
  def _mark_seen(self, doc: UnifiedDocument)  # Mark document as seen for deduplication.
  def _convert_pubmed(self, article: PubMedArticle) -> UnifiedDocument  # Convert PubMed article to unified format.
  def _convert_scholar(self, article: ScholarArticle) -> UnifiedDocument  # Convert Semantic Scholar article to unified format.
  def _convert_arxiv(self, paper: ArxivPaper) -> UnifiedDocument  # Convert arXiv paper to unified format.
  def _convert_openalex(self, work: OpenAlexWork) -> UnifiedDocument  # Convert OpenAlex work to unified format.
  def _convert_clinicaltrial(self, trial: ClinicalTrial) -> UnifiedDocument  # Convert ClinicalTrials.gov record to unified format.
  def _convert_europepmc(self, article: EuropePMCArticle) -> UnifiedDocument  # Convert Europe PMC article to unified format.
  def ingest_pubmed(
        self,
        years_back: int = 10,
        max_per_query: int = 5000,
        include_full_text: bool = True
    ) -> AsyncIterator[UnifiedDocument]  # Ingest from PubMed.
  def ingest_semantic_scholar(
        self,
        max_per_query: int = 1000,
        year_start: int = 2014
    ) -> AsyncIterator[UnifiedDocument]  # Ingest from Semantic Scholar.
  def ingest_arxiv(
        self,
        max_results: int = 5000,
        years_back: int = 5
    ) -> AsyncIterator[UnifiedDocument]  # Ingest from arXiv.
  def ingest_openalex(
        self,
        from_year: int = 2014,
        max_results: int = 50000
    ) -> AsyncIterator[UnifiedDocument]  # Ingest from OpenAlex.
  def ingest_clinicaltrials(
        self,
        max_results: int = 500,
        status_filter: Optional[list[str]] = None,
    ) -> AsyncIterator[UnifiedDocument]  # Ingest EEG-relevant clinical trials from ClinicalTrials.gov.
  def ingest_europepmc(
        self,
        max_results: int = 1000,
        min_year: int = 2010,
        fetch_full_text: bool = False,
    ) -> AsyncIterator[UnifiedDocument]  # Ingest EEG open-access articles from Europe PMC.
  def run_full_ingestion(
        self,
        sources: list[str] = None,
        save_interval: int = 1000,
    ) -> dict  # Run full ingestion from all sources.
  def _save_documents(self, docs: list[UnifiedDocument], output_file: Path)  # Save documents to JSONL file.
  def reset_deduplication(self)  # Reset deduplication tracking for a fresh run.
def main()  # CLI entry point for ingestion pipeline.

class MemoryType(Enum)
class MemoryEntry
  def __post_init__(self)  # Generate unique ID if not provided and validate all fields
  def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]  # Convert to dictionary for storage
  def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "MemoryEntry"  # Create from dictionary with validation
  def is_expired(self, ttl_seconds: float) -> bool  # Check if entry is expired
  def update_relevance_score(self, new_score: float, reason: Optional[str] = None) -> None  # Update relevance score with validation
  def get_age_seconds(self) -> float  # Get entry age in seconds
class ShortTermMemory
  def __init__(
        self,
        max_entries: int = 50,
        ttl_seconds: float = 3600.0,  # REQ-MEM-026: Default 1 hour in seconds
        logger: Optional[logging.Logger] = None
    )  # Initialize short-term memory
  def add(self, entry: MemoryEntry) -> None  # Add entry to short-term memory with validation
  def get(self, entry_id: str) -> Optional[MemoryEntry]  # Get entry by ID with expiration checking
  def get_recent(self, n: int = 5) -> List[MemoryEntry]  # Get N most recent entries
  def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        memory_type: Optional[MemoryType] = None,
        top_k: int = 5,
        min_similarity: float = 0.1

        self,

    )  # Initialize connection pool.

  def release(self, conn: T) -> None  # Release a connection back to the pool.

  def close_all(self) -> None  # Close all connections in the pool.
